1	/**
2	 * @cipherbox/sdk - Shared-write operations
3	 *
4	 * Stateless functions for write operations on shared folders.
5	 * Extracted from: apps/web/src/hooks/useSharedNavigation.ts (write handlers)
6	 *
7	 * Each function takes explicit context (keys, IPNS state, callbacks) and
8	 * returns updated state. No React/Zustand/browser dependencies.
9	 *
10	 * Key wrapping convention for shared folders:
11	 * - FolderEntry/FilePointer fields wrap keys with the OWNER's public key
12	 *   (so the owner can access their own data)
13	 * - share_keys entries wrap keys with the RECIPIENT's public key
14	 *   (so the share recipient can access)
15	 */
16	
17	import {
18	  encryptAesGcm,
19	  generateFileKey,
20	  generateIv,
21	  wrapKey,
22	  bytesToHex,
23	  generateRandomBytes,
24	  generateEd25519Keypair,
25	  deriveIpnsName,
26	} from '@cipherbox/crypto';
27	import {
28	  generateFileIpnsKeypair,
29	  encryptFolderMetadata,
30	  encryptFileMetadata,
31	  createIpnsRecord,
32	  marshalIpnsRecord,
33	  type FolderChild,
34	  type FolderEntry,
35	  type FilePointer,
36	  type FileMetadata,
37	  type FolderMetadata,
38	} from '@cipherbox/core';
39	import {
40	  updateFolderMetadataAndPublish,
41	  addToIpfs,
42	  batchPublishIpnsRecords,
43	  createAndPublishIpnsRecord,
44	  resolveFileMetadata,
45	  updateFileMetadata,
46	  unpinFromIpfs,
47	} from '@cipherbox/sdk-core';
48	import type { SdkContext } from '@cipherbox/sdk-core';
49	import type { ShareKeyEntryDtoKeyType } from '@cipherbox/api-client';
50	
51	/** Re-export the canonical share key type for consumers */
52	export type ShareKeyType = ShareKeyEntryDtoKeyType;
53	
54	/**
55	 * Context for shared-write operations on a folder.
56	 * Provides all state needed to modify folder contents and publish changes.
57	 */
58	export type SharedWriteContext = {
59	  /** SDK context for IPFS/IPNS API access */
60	  ctx: SdkContext;
61	  /** Decrypted AES-256 folder key for the current folder */
62	  folderKey: Uint8Array;
63	  /** Ed25519 private key for signing IPNS publishes */
64	  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
65	  /** IPNS name of the current folder */
66	  ipnsName: string;
67	  /** Current sequence number for conflict detection */
68	  sequenceNumber: bigint;
69	  /** Current folder children */
70	  children: FolderChild[];
71	  /** Sharer's secp256k1 public key (keys in FolderEntry/FilePointer wrap for owner) */
72	  ownerPublicKey: Uint8Array;
73	  /** Current user's secp256k1 public key (keys in share_keys wrap for recipient) */
74	  recipientPublicKey: Uint8Array;
75	  /** Share ID for addShareKeysFn calls */
76	  shareId: string;
77	  /** Callback to add share keys for the recipient */
78	  addShareKeysFn: (
79	    shareId: string,
80	    keys: Array<{
81	      keyType: ShareKeyType;
82	      itemId: string;
83	      encryptedKey: string;
84	    }>
85	  ) => Promise<void>;
86	};
87	
88	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
89	// Function 1: uploadToSharedFolder
90	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
91	
92	/**
93	 * Upload a file to a write-shared folder.
94	 *
95	 * Encrypts the file content, creates per-file IPNS metadata, adds the
96	 * FilePointer to folder children, publishes the updated folder, and
97	 * registers share_keys for the recipient.
98	 *
99	 * Key wrapping:
100	 * - fileKey wrapped with ownerPublicKey -> FilePointer.fileKeyEncrypted (owner access)
101	 * - fileKey wrapped with recipientPublicKey -> share_keys file key (recipient access)
102	 * - IPNS private key wrapped with ownerPublicKey -> FilePointer.ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted
103	 * - IPNS private key wrapped with recipientPublicKey -> share_keys file-ipns key
104	 */
105	export async function uploadToSharedFolder(
106	  swCtx: SharedWriteContext,
107	  params: { data: Uint8Array; fileName: string; mimeType?: string }
108	): Promise<{
109	  publishedChildren: FolderChild[];
110	  newSequenceNumber: bigint;
111	  filePointer: FilePointer;
112	}> {
113	  const fileKey = generateFileKey();
114	  const iv = generateIv();
115	
116	  try {
117	    // 1. Encrypt file content
118	    const ciphertext = await encryptAesGcm(params.data, fileKey, iv);
119	
120	    // 2. Upload encrypted content to IPFS
121	    const { cid: contentCid } = await addToIpfs(swCtx.ctx, new Uint8Array(ciphertext));
122	
123	    // 3. Wrap fileKey with owner's public key (for FilePointer)
124	    const ownerWrappedFileKey = await wrapKey(fileKey, swCtx.ownerPublicKey);
125	    const fileKeyEncrypted = bytesToHex(ownerWrappedFileKey);
126	
127	    // 4. Generate IPNS keypair for file metadata
128	    const fileId = crypto.randomUUID();
129	    const mimeType = params.mimeType ?? 'application/octet-stream';
130	    const ipnsKeypair = await generateFileIpnsKeypair();
131	
132	    try {
133	      // 5. Wrap IPNS private key for owner (FilePointer) and recipient (share_keys)
134	      const ownerWrappedIpnsKey = await wrapKey(ipnsKeypair.privateKey, swCtx.ownerPublicKey);
135	      const ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted = bytesToHex(ownerWrappedIpnsKey);
136	      const recipientWrappedIpnsKey = await wrapKey(
137	        ipnsKeypair.privateKey,
138	        swCtx.recipientPublicKey
139	      );
140	
141	      // 6. Create and encrypt file metadata
142	      const now = Date.now();
143	      const fileMeta: FileMetadata = {
144	        version: 'v1',
145	        cid: contentCid,
146	        fileKeyEncrypted,
147	        fileIv: bytesToHex(iv),
148	        size: params.data.length,
149	        mimeType,
150	        encryptionMode: 'GCM',
151	        createdAt: now,
152	        modifiedAt: now,
153	      };
154	      const encrypted = await encryptFileMetadata(fileMeta, swCtx.folderKey);
155	
156	      // 7. Upload encrypted metadata to IPFS
157	      const jsonStr = JSON.stringify(encrypted);
158	      const metadataBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(jsonStr);
159	      const { cid: metadataCid } = await addToIpfs(swCtx.ctx, metadataBytes);
160	
161	      // 8. Create and publish file IPNS record
162	      // Use createAndPublishIpnsRecord for single file IPNS, then batch publish
163	      const ipnsLifetimeMs = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
164	      const record = await createIpnsRecord(
165	        ipnsKeypair.privateKey,
166	        `/ipfs/${metadataCid}`,
167	        1n,
168	        ipnsLifetimeMs
169	      );
170	      const recordBytes = marshalIpnsRecord(record);
171	      let binary = '';
172	      for (let i = 0; i < recordBytes.length; i++) {
173	        binary += String.fromCharCode(recordBytes[i]);
174	      }
175	      const recordBase64 = btoa(binary);
176	
177	      await batchPublishIpnsRecords(
178	        [
179	          {
180	            ipnsName: ipnsKeypair.ipnsName,
181	            recordBase64,
182	            metadataCid,
183	            recordType: 'file' as const,
184	          },
185	        ],
186	        swCtx.ctx
187	      );
188	
189	      // 9. Build FilePointer (owner-wrapped keys)
190	      const filePointer: FilePointer = {
191	        type: 'file',
192	        id: fileId,
193	        name: params.fileName,
194	        fileMetaIpnsName: ipnsKeypair.ipnsName,
195	        ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted,
196	        createdAt: now,
197	        modifiedAt: now,
198	      };
199	
200	      // 10. Add file to folder children and publish
201	      const updatedChildren = [...swCtx.children, filePointer];
202	      const { newSequenceNumber, publishedChildren } = await updateFolderMetadataAndPublish({
203	        children: updatedChildren,
204	        baseChildren: swCtx.children,
205	        folderKey: swCtx.folderKey,
206	        ipnsPrivateKey: swCtx.ipnsPrivateKey,
207	        ipnsName: swCtx.ipnsName,
208	        sequenceNumber: swCtx.sequenceNumber,
209	        ctx: swCtx.ctx,
210	      });
211	
212	      // 11. Add share_keys for recipient (file + file-ipns)
213	      const recipientWrappedFileKey = await wrapKey(fileKey, swCtx.recipientPublicKey);
214	      try {
215	        await swCtx.addShareKeysFn(swCtx.shareId, [
216	          { keyType: 'file', itemId: fileId, encryptedKey: bytesToHex(recipientWrappedFileKey) },
217	          {
218	            keyType: 'file-ipns',
219	            itemId: fileId,
220	            encryptedKey: bytesToHex(recipientWrappedIpnsKey),
221	          },
222	        ]);
223	      } catch (err) {
224	        console.warn('[shared-write] Failed to add share_keys for uploaded file:', err);
225	      }
226	
227	      return { publishedChildren, newSequenceNumber, filePointer };
228	    } finally {
229	      ipnsKeypair.privateKey.fill(0);
230	    }
231	  } finally {
232	    fileKey.fill(0);
233	  }
234	}
235	
236	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
237	// Function 2: createSharedSubfolder
238	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
239	
240	/**
241	 * Create a subfolder in a write-shared folder.
242	 *
243	 * Generates an Ed25519 keypair and AES-256 folder key, wraps with owner's
244	 * key for FolderEntry, publishes empty subfolder IPNS, adds FolderEntry to
245	 * parent, publishes parent, and registers share_keys for the recipient.
246	 */
247	export async function createSharedSubfolder(
248	  swCtx: SharedWriteContext,
249	  params: { name: string }
250	): Promise<{
251	  publishedChildren: FolderChild[];
252	  newSequenceNumber: bigint;
253	  folderEntry: FolderEntry;
254	}> {
255	  // Generate Ed25519 keypair for the subfolder IPNS
256	  const keypair = await generateEd25519Keypair();
257	  const subfolderIpnsName = await deriveIpnsName(keypair.publicKey);
258	
259	  // Generate AES-256 folder key for the subfolder
260	  const subfolderKey = generateRandomBytes(32);
261	
262	  try {
263	    // 1. Wrap keys with owner's public key (for FolderEntry)
264	    const wrappedFolderKey = await wrapKey(subfolderKey, swCtx.ownerPublicKey);
265	    const wrappedIpnsKey = await wrapKey(keypair.privateKey, swCtx.ownerPublicKey);
266	
267	    // 2. Create and encrypt empty folder metadata
268	    const subfolderMetadata: FolderMetadata = { version: 'v2', children: [] };
269	    const encrypted = await encryptFolderMetadata(subfolderMetadata, subfolderKey);
270	    const jsonStr = JSON.stringify(encrypted);
271	    const encryptedBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(jsonStr);
272	    const { cid: subfolderCid } = await addToIpfs(swCtx.ctx, encryptedBytes);
273	
274	    // 3. Publish subfolder IPNS
275	    await createAndPublishIpnsRecord({
276	      ipnsPrivateKey: keypair.privateKey,
277	      ipnsName: subfolderIpnsName,
278	      metadataCid: subfolderCid,
279	      sequenceNumber: 1n,
280	      ctx: swCtx.ctx,
281	    });
282	
283	    // 4. Create FolderEntry for the parent
284	    const folderId = crypto.randomUUID();
285	    const folderEntry: FolderEntry = {
286	      type: 'folder',
287	      id: folderId,
288	      name: params.name,
289	      ipnsName: subfolderIpnsName,
290	      ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: bytesToHex(wrappedIpnsKey),
291	      folderKeyEncrypted: bytesToHex(wrappedFolderKey),
292	      createdAt: Date.now(),
293	      modifiedAt: Date.now(),
294	    };
295	
296	    // 5. Add to parent folder and publish
297	    const updatedChildren = [...swCtx.children, folderEntry];
298	    const { newSequenceNumber, publishedChildren } = await updateFolderMetadataAndPublish({
299	      children: updatedChildren,
300	      baseChildren: swCtx.children,
301	      folderKey: swCtx.folderKey,
302	      ipnsPrivateKey: swCtx.ipnsPrivateKey,
303	      ipnsName: swCtx.ipnsName,
304	      sequenceNumber: swCtx.sequenceNumber,
305	      ctx: swCtx.ctx,
306	    });
307	
308	    // 6. Add share_keys for recipient (folder + folder-ipns)
309	    const recipientWrappedFolderKey = await wrapKey(subfolderKey, swCtx.recipientPublicKey);
310	    const recipientWrappedIpnsKey = await wrapKey(keypair.privateKey, swCtx.recipientPublicKey);
311	    try {
312	      await swCtx.addShareKeysFn(swCtx.shareId, [
313	        {
314	          keyType: 'folder',
315	          itemId: folderId,
316	          encryptedKey: bytesToHex(recipientWrappedFolderKey),
317	        },
318	        {
319	          keyType: 'folder-ipns',
320	          itemId: folderId,
321	          encryptedKey: bytesToHex(recipientWrappedIpnsKey),
322	        },
323	      ]);
324	    } catch (err) {
325	      console.warn('[shared-write] Failed to add share_keys for subfolder:', err);
326	    }
327	
328	    return { publishedChildren, newSequenceNumber, folderEntry };
329	  } finally {
330	    subfolderKey.fill(0);
331	    keypair.privateKey.fill(0);
332	  }
333	}
334	
335	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
336	// Function 3: renameInSharedFolder
337	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
338	
339	/**
340	 * Rename an item in a write-shared folder.
341	 *
342	 * Maps over children to update the name and modifiedAt, then publishes
343	 * the updated folder metadata.
344	 */
345	export async function renameInSharedFolder(
346	  swCtx: SharedWriteContext,
347	  params: { itemId: string; newName: string }
348	): Promise<{
349	  publishedChildren: FolderChild[];
350	  newSequenceNumber: bigint;
351	}> {
352	  const updatedChildren = swCtx.children.map((child) =>
353	    child.id === params.itemId ? { ...child, name: params.newName, modifiedAt: Date.now() } : child
354	  );
355	
356	  const { newSequenceNumber, publishedChildren } = await updateFolderMetadataAndPublish({
357	    children: updatedChildren,
358	    baseChildren: swCtx.children,
359	    folderKey: swCtx.folderKey,
360	    ipnsPrivateKey: swCtx.ipnsPrivateKey,
361	    ipnsName: swCtx.ipnsName,
362	    sequenceNumber: swCtx.sequenceNumber,
363	    ctx: swCtx.ctx,
364	  });
365	
366	  return { publishedChildren, newSequenceNumber };
367	}
368	
369	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
370	// Function 4: deleteFromSharedFolder
371	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
372	
373	/**
374	 * Delete an item from a write-shared folder.
375	 *
376	 * Filters the item from children and publishes the updated folder metadata.
377	 */
378	export async function deleteFromSharedFolder(
379	  swCtx: SharedWriteContext,
380	  params: { itemId: string }
381	): Promise<{
382	  publishedChildren: FolderChild[];
383	  newSequenceNumber: bigint;
384	}> {
385	  const updatedChildren = swCtx.children.filter((child) => child.id !== params.itemId);
386	
387	  const { newSequenceNumber, publishedChildren } = await updateFolderMetadataAndPublish({
388	    children: updatedChildren,
389	    baseChildren: swCtx.children,
390	    folderKey: swCtx.folderKey,
391	    ipnsPrivateKey: swCtx.ipnsPrivateKey,
392	    ipnsName: swCtx.ipnsName,
393	    sequenceNumber: swCtx.sequenceNumber,
394	    ctx: swCtx.ctx,
395	  });
396	
397	  return { publishedChildren, newSequenceNumber };
398	}
399	
400	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
401	// Function 5: updateSharedFile
402	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
403	
404	/**
405	 * Update a file's content in a write-shared context.
406	 *
407	 * Encrypts new content, uploads to IPFS, resolves current file metadata,
408	 * updates the file metadata IPNS record, and refreshes the recipient's
409	 * share_key for the file.
410	 *
411	 * Does NOT modify folder metadata (the FilePointer stays the same).
412	 */
413	export async function updateSharedFile(params: {
414	  ctx: SdkContext;
415	  folderKey: Uint8Array;
416	  ownerPublicKey: Uint8Array;
417	  recipientPublicKey: Uint8Array;
418	  shareId: string;
419	  addShareKeysFn: (
420	    shareId: string,
421	    keys: Array<{
422	      keyType: ShareKeyType;
423	      itemId: string;
424	      encryptedKey: string;
425	    }>
426	  ) => Promise<void>;
427	  filePointer: FilePointer;
428	  newContent: Uint8Array;
429	  /** Callback to get the file's IPNS private key (checks share_keys then FilePointer fallback) */
430	  getFileIpnsKeyFn: (itemId: string) => Promise<Uint8Array | null>;
431	}): Promise<void> {
432	  // 1. Encrypt new content
433	  const fileKey = generateFileKey();
434	  const iv = generateIv();
435	
436	  try {
437	    const ciphertext = await encryptAesGcm(params.newContent, fileKey, iv);
438	
439	    // 2. Upload encrypted content to IPFS
440	    const { cid: contentCid } = await addToIpfs(params.ctx, new Uint8Array(ciphertext));
441	
442	    // 3. Wrap fileKey with owner's public key
443	    const ownerWrappedKey = await wrapKey(fileKey, params.ownerPublicKey);
444	    const fileKeyEncrypted = bytesToHex(ownerWrappedKey);
445	
446	    // 4. Get the file's IPNS private key
447	    const ipnsPrivKey = await params.getFileIpnsKeyFn(params.filePointer.id);
448	    if (!ipnsPrivKey) {
449	      throw new Error('Cannot update: no file IPNS key available');
450	    }
451	
452	    try {
453	      // 5. Resolve current file metadata
454	      const { metadata: currentMeta } = await resolveFileMetadata(
455	        params.filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
456	        params.folderKey,
457	        params.ctx
458	      );
459	
460	      // 6. Update file metadata (publishes internally via CAS — Plan 03)
461	      const { prunedCids } = await updateFileMetadata({
462	        fileIpnsPrivateKey: ipnsPrivKey,
463	        fileMetaIpnsName: params.filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
464	        folderKey: params.folderKey,
465	        currentMetadata: currentMeta,
466	        updates: {
467	          cid: contentCid,
468	          fileKeyEncrypted,
469	          fileIv: bytesToHex(iv),
470	          size: params.newContent.length,
471	          encryptionMode: 'GCM',
472	        },
473	        createVersion: false,
474	        ctx: params.ctx,
475	      });
476	
477	      // 7. Unpin version-history CIDs pruned by the per-file version cap, mirroring
478	      // the owner path (useFileOperations.ts). Fire-and-forget: the Phase-42
479	      // server-side guarded-unpin (ownership + cross-user refcount) blocks any actual
480	      // cross-user unpin and returns 403 for a CID the recipient does not own; that
481	      // rejection is caught and logged, never propagated, so a share write never fails
482	      // on an unpin (T-47-04).
483	      for (const cid of prunedCids) {
484	        unpinFromIpfs(params.ctx, cid).catch((err) => {
485	          console.warn('[shared-write] Unpin pruned CID failed:', err);
486	        });
487	      }
488	
489	      // 8. Update share_key for recipient
490	      const recipientWrappedKey = await wrapKey(fileKey, params.recipientPublicKey);
491	      try {
492	        await params.addShareKeysFn(params.shareId, [
493	          {
494	            keyType: 'file',
495	            itemId: params.filePointer.id,
496	            encryptedKey: bytesToHex(recipientWrappedKey),
497	          },
498	        ]);
499	      } catch (err) {
500	        console.warn('[shared-write] Failed to update share_key after file edit:', err);
501	      }
502	    } finally {
503	      ipnsPrivKey.fill(0);
504	    }
505	  } finally {
506	    fileKey.fill(0);
507	  }
508	}
509	
510	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
511	// Function 6: updateSharePermission
512	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
513	
514	/**
515	 * Update a share's permission level.
516	 *
517	 * Thin callback wrapper consistent with the existing revokeShare pattern.
518	 */
519	export async function updateSharePermission(params: {
520	  shareId: string;
521	  permission: string;
522	  encryptedIpnsKey?: string;
523	  updatePermissionFn: (
524	    shareId: string,
525	    body: { permission: string; encryptedIpnsKey?: string }
526	  ) => Promise<void>;
527	}): Promise<void> {
528	  await params.updatePermissionFn(params.shareId, {
529	    permission: params.permission,
530	    encryptedIpnsKey: params.encryptedIpnsKey,
531	  });
532	}
533

1	/**
2	 * Generic CAS-retry helper for IPNS publish operations.
3	 *
4	 * Owns the resolve→encrypt→upload→CAS→409→merge→retry→ConflictError skeleton
5	 * for both file and folder publish paths. Domain-specific encode, decode, and
6	 * merge logic is injected as callbacks.
7	 *
8	 * Security: publishWithCas NEVER zeroes key material — callers are responsible
9	 * for zeroing after the call returns (T-47-01).
10	 */
11	
12	import type { SdkContext } from './types';
13	import { createAndPublishIpnsRecord, resolveIpnsRecord } from './ipns';
14	import { is409, ConflictError } from './errors';
15	
16	// Exponential backoff constants
17	const BACKOFF_BASE_MS = 100;
18	const BACKOFF_CAP_MS = 1500;
19	
20	/** Exponential backoff with ±50% jitter. */
21	function retryDelayMs(attempt: number): number {
22	  const base = Math.min(BACKOFF_BASE_MS * 2 ** attempt, BACKOFF_CAP_MS);
23	  return base * (0.5 + Math.random()); // ±50% jitter => [0.5x, 1.5x)
24	}
25	
26	/**
27	 * Generic CAS-retry publish helper.
28	 *
29	 * On success returns the published CID, new sequence number, final local data,
30	 * and any pruned CIDs accumulated across merge rounds.
31	 *
32	 * On 409 conflict: re-resolves authoritatively, fetches+decodes remote data,
33	 * calls merge(base, local, remote), then retries with the merged data.
34	 *
35	 * Throws ConflictError after maxAttempts unsuccessful publishes.
36	 * Rethrows non-409 errors immediately without retry.
37	 */
38	export async function publishWithCas<TData>(params: {
39	  ipnsName: string;
40	  ipnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
41	  ipnsPublicKey?: Uint8Array;
42	  sequenceNumber: bigint;
43	  ctx: SdkContext;
44	  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
45	  keyEpoch?: number;
46	  maxAttempts: number;
47	  backoff: boolean;
48	  /** Encode local state to IPFS and return the resulting CID. */
49	  encodeAndUpload: (local: TData) => Promise<string>;
50	  /** Decode remote data from IPFS using the given CID. */
51	  decodeRemote: (cid: string) => Promise<TData>;
52	  /**
53	   * Three-way merge returning merged data and optional pruned CIDs.
54	   *
55	   * `base` is `undefined` when the caller omits `baseData` (e.g. the latest-wins
56	   * file path, which ignores `base`); merge implementations that read `base`
57	   * must defend against `undefined`.
58	   */
59	  merge: (
60	    base: TData | undefined,
61	    local: TData,
62	    remote: TData
63	  ) => { merged: TData; prunedCids?: string[] };
64	  /** Initial local data for the first publish attempt. */
65	  localData: TData;
66	  /** Base snapshot for three-way merge. */
67	  baseData?: TData;
68	}): Promise<{
69	  cid: string;
70	  newSequenceNumber: bigint;
71	  publishedData: TData;
72	  prunedCids: string[];
73	}> {
74	  let currentSeq = params.sequenceNumber;
75	  let localData = params.localData;
76	  let lastRemoteSeq: bigint = params.sequenceNumber;
77	  let prunedCids: string[] = [];
78	
79	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < params.maxAttempts; attempt++) {
80	    // 1. Encode + upload (domain-specific, injected)
81	    const cid = await params.encodeAndUpload(localData);
82	    const newSeq = currentSeq + 1n;
83	
84	    try {
85	      // 2. CAS publish with expectedSequenceNumber guard
86	      await createAndPublishIpnsRecord({
87	        ipnsPrivateKey: params.ipnsPrivateKey,
88	        ipnsPublicKey: params.ipnsPublicKey,
89	        ipnsName: params.ipnsName,
90	        metadataCid: cid,
91	        sequenceNumber: newSeq,
92	        encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: params.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
93	        keyEpoch: params.keyEpoch,
94	        expectedSequenceNumber: currentSeq.toString(),
95	        ctx: params.ctx,
96	      });
97	      return { cid, newSequenceNumber: newSeq, publishedData: localData, prunedCids };
98	    } catch (err) {
99	      if (!is409(err)) throw err;
100	
101	      // 3. Re-resolve authoritatively — ignore seq hint in error body
102	      const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(params.ipnsName, params.ctx);
103	      if (!resolved) {
104	        throw new ConflictError(params.ipnsName, attempt + 1, lastRemoteSeq);
105	      }
106	      currentSeq = resolved.sequenceNumber;
107	      lastRemoteSeq = resolved.sequenceNumber;
108	
109	      // 4. Fetch + decode remote
110	      const remoteData = await params.decodeRemote(resolved.cid);
111	
112	      // 5. Three-way merge (domain-specific, injected)
113	      const { merged, prunedCids: extraPruned } = params.merge(
114	        params.baseData,
115	        localData,
116	        remoteData
117	      );
118	      localData = merged;
119	      prunedCids = [...new Set([...prunedCids, ...(extraPruned ?? [])])];
120	
121	      // 6. After the final attempt, throw ConflictError
122	      if (attempt === params.maxAttempts - 1) {
123	        throw new ConflictError(params.ipnsName, params.maxAttempts, lastRemoteSeq);
124	      }
125	
126	      // 7. Backoff + jitter before next attempt
127	      if (params.backoff) {
128	        await new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, retryDelayMs(attempt)));
129	      }
130	    }
131	  }
132	
133	  // Unreachable — ConflictError is thrown inside the loop above on exhaustion
134	  throw new ConflictError(params.ipnsName, params.maxAttempts, lastRemoteSeq);
135	}
136

1	/**
2	 * File Metadata Service - Per-file IPNS operations
3	 *
4	 * Extracted from: apps/web/src/services/file-metadata.service.ts
5	 * Change: teeKeys passed explicitly instead of read from useAuthStore.
6	 * Change: IPFS/IPNS operations use sdk-core internal modules instead of web app imports.
7	 */
8	
9	import {
10	  generateFileIpnsKeypair,
11	  encryptFileMetadata,
12	  decryptFileMetadata,
13	  createIpnsRecord,
14	  marshalIpnsRecord,
15	  type FileMetadata,
16	  type EncryptedFileMetadata,
17	  type VersionEntry,
18	} from '@cipherbox/core';
19	import { wrapKey, bytesToHex, hexToBytes } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
20	import type { SdkContext, TeeKeys } from '../types';
21	import { addToIpfs, fetchFromIpfs } from '../ipfs';
22	import { resolveIpnsRecord } from '../ipns';
23	import { publishWithCas } from '../cas';
24	
25	/** IPNS record lifetime: 24 hours in milliseconds */
26	const IPNS_LIFETIME_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
27	
28	/** Maximum number of past versions retained per file (VER-04) */
29	const MAX_VERSIONS_PER_FILE = 10;
30	
31	/** Safe base64 encoding that avoids call stack overflow from spread operator */
32	function uint8ToBase64(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
33	  let binary = '';
34	  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
35	    binary += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);
36	  }
37	  return btoa(binary);
38	}
39	
40	/** Record payload ready for batch publish */
41	export type FileIpnsRecordPayload = {
42	  ipnsName: string;
43	  recordBase64: string;
44	  publicKey?: string;
45	  metadataCid: string;
46	  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey?: string;
47	  keyEpoch?: number;
48	};
49	
50	/**
51	 * Merge two VersionEntry arrays into a single deduped, sorted, capped result.
52	 *
53	 * Algorithm (RESEARCH Pattern 5):
54	 * 1. combined = [...a, ...b]
55	 * 2. dedupe by `cid` (first occurrence wins via Set filter)
56	 * 3. sort by `timestamp` DESC (newest first)
57	 * 4. `versions` = first `maxVersions` entries; `prunedCids` = remaining cids
58	 *
59	 * @param a - First array (or undefined)
60	 * @param b - Second array (or undefined)
61	 * @param maxVersions - Maximum number of versions to keep
62	 * @returns merged versions and pruned cids
63	 */
64	export function mergeVersions(
65	  a: VersionEntry[] | undefined,
66	  b: VersionEntry[] | undefined,
67	  maxVersions: number
68	): { versions: VersionEntry[]; prunedCids: string[] } {
69	  const combined = [...(a ?? []), ...(b ?? [])];
70	
71	  // Dedupe by cid — first occurrence wins
72	  const seen = new Set<string>();
73	  const deduped = combined.filter((v) => {
74	    if (seen.has(v.cid)) return false;
75	    seen.add(v.cid);
76	    return true;
77	  });
78	
79	  // Sort newest first
80	  deduped.sort((x, y) => y.timestamp - x.timestamp);
81	
82	  const versions = deduped.slice(0, maxVersions);
83	  const prunedCids = deduped.slice(maxVersions).map((v) => v.cid);
84	
85	  return { versions, prunedCids };
86	}
87	
88	/**
89	 * Create a per-file IPNS metadata record.
90	 *
91	 * Generates a random Ed25519 IPNS keypair for the file, encrypts file metadata
92	 * with the parent folder's key, uploads to IPFS, and creates a signed IPNS record.
93	 *
94	 * @returns File IPNS name, record payload for batch publish, and ECIES-wrapped IPNS private key
95	 */
96	export async function createFileMetadata(params: {
97	  fileId: string;
98	  cid: string;
99	  fileKeyEncrypted: string;
100	  fileIv: string;
101	  size: number;
102	  mimeType: string;
103	  folderKey: Uint8Array;
104	  userPublicKey: Uint8Array;
105	  ctx: SdkContext;
106	  teeKeys?: TeeKeys;
107	  encryptionMode?: 'GCM' | 'CTR';
108	}): Promise<{
109	  fileMetaIpnsName: string;
110	  ipnsRecord: FileIpnsRecordPayload;
111	  ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: string;
112	}> {
113	  // 1. Generate random Ed25519 IPNS keypair for this file
114	  const ipnsKeypair = await generateFileIpnsKeypair();
115	
116	  // All key-using operations inside try/finally to guarantee zeroization
117	  try {
118	    // 2. ECIES-wrap the IPNS private key with user's public key for storage in FilePointer
119	    const wrappedIpnsKey = await wrapKey(ipnsKeypair.privateKey, params.userPublicKey);
120	    const ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted = bytesToHex(wrappedIpnsKey);
121	
122	    const now = Date.now();
123	    const metadata: FileMetadata = {
124	      version: 'v1',
125	      cid: params.cid,
126	      fileKeyEncrypted: params.fileKeyEncrypted,
127	      fileIv: params.fileIv,
128	      size: params.size,
129	      mimeType: params.mimeType,
130	      encryptionMode: params.encryptionMode ?? 'GCM',
131	      createdAt: now,
132	      modifiedAt: now,
133	    };
134	
135	    // 4. Encrypt with parent folderKey
136	    const encrypted: EncryptedFileMetadata = await encryptFileMetadata(metadata, params.folderKey);
137	
138	    // 5. Upload encrypted metadata to IPFS
139	    const jsonStr = JSON.stringify(encrypted);
140	    const encryptedBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(jsonStr);
141	    const { cid: metadataCid } = await addToIpfs(params.ctx, encryptedBytes);
142	
143	    // 6. Create IPNS record (sequence number 1 for new records)
144	    const record = await createIpnsRecord(
145	      ipnsKeypair.privateKey,
146	      `/ipfs/${metadataCid}`,
147	      1n,
148	      IPNS_LIFETIME_MS
149	    );
150	
151	    // 7. Marshal and base64 encode the record
152	    const recordBytes = marshalIpnsRecord(record);
153	    const recordBase64 = uint8ToBase64(recordBytes);
154	
155	    // 8. TEE enrollment: encrypt IPNS private key with TEE public key
156	    let teeEncryptedIpnsPrivateKey: string | undefined;
157	    let keyEpoch: number | undefined;
158	
159	    if (params.teeKeys?.currentPublicKey) {
160	      const teePublicKey = hexToBytes(params.teeKeys.currentPublicKey);
161	      const encryptedKey = await wrapKey(ipnsKeypair.privateKey, teePublicKey);
162	      teeEncryptedIpnsPrivateKey = bytesToHex(encryptedKey);
163	      keyEpoch = params.teeKeys.currentEpoch;
164	    }
165	
166	    return {
167	      fileMetaIpnsName: ipnsKeypair.ipnsName,
168	      ipnsRecord: {
169	        ipnsName: ipnsKeypair.ipnsName,
170	        recordBase64,
171	        publicKey: uint8ToBase64(ipnsKeypair.publicKey),
172	        metadataCid,
173	        encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: teeEncryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
174	        keyEpoch,
175	      },
176	      ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted,
177	    };
178	  } finally {
179	    // Zero the private key on all exit paths (success and failure)
180	    ipnsKeypair.privateKey.fill(0);
181	  }
182	}
183	
184	/**
185	 * Resolve a file's per-IPNS metadata record.
186	 *
187	 * Resolves the file's IPNS name to get the current metadata CID,
188	 * fetches the encrypted metadata from IPFS, and decrypts with the
189	 * parent folder's key.
190	 *
191	 * @returns Decrypted file metadata and the resolved metadata CID
192	 */
193	export async function resolveFileMetadata(
194	  fileMetaIpnsName: string,
195	  folderKey: Uint8Array,
196	  ctx: SdkContext
197	): Promise<{ metadata: FileMetadata; metadataCid: string }> {
198	  const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(fileMetaIpnsName, ctx);
199	
200	  if (!resolved) {
201	    throw new Error('File metadata IPNS not found');
202	  }
203	
204	  const metadata = await fetchAndDecryptFileMetadata(resolved.cid, folderKey, ctx);
205	
206	  return { metadata, metadataCid: resolved.cid };
207	}
208	
209	/**
210	 * Update an existing file's per-IPNS metadata record.
211	 *
212	 * Publishes with CAS (expectedSequenceNumber) to close the TOCTOU window (D-06).
213	 * On 409 conflict, applies latest-wins semantics by modifiedAt: the winner keeps
214	 * its content pointer; the loser's content is preserved as a VersionEntry (D-07).
215	 * versions[] union-merged/deduped/sorted/capped by maxVersionsPerFile (default 10).
216	 * Throws ConflictError after two total publish attempts.
217	 *
218	 * Contract change from original: now publishes internally via createAndPublishIpnsRecord
219	 * and returns { ipnsName, metadataCid, newSequenceNumber, prunedCids }.
220	 * The old return shape { ipnsRecord, prunedCids } is replaced. Plan 04 callers
221	 * (useFileOperations.ts:416, shared-write.ts:450) must be updated to consume this shape.
222	 *
223	 * @returns Published IPNS name, CID, sequence number, and pruned version CIDs
224	 */
225	export async function updateFileMetadata(params: {
226	  fileIpnsPrivateKey: Uint8Array;
227	  fileMetaIpnsName: string;
228	  folderKey: Uint8Array;
229	  currentMetadata: FileMetadata;
230	  updates: Partial<
231	    Pick<FileMetadata, 'cid' | 'fileKeyEncrypted' | 'fileIv' | 'size' | 'encryptionMode'>
232	  >;
233	  createVersion: boolean;
234	  maxVersionsPerFile?: number;
235	  ctx: SdkContext;
236	}): Promise<{
237	  ipnsName: string;
238	  metadataCid: string;
239	  newSequenceNumber: bigint;
240	  prunedCids: string[];
241	}> {
242	  const maxVersions = params.maxVersionsPerFile ?? MAX_VERSIONS_PER_FILE;
243	
244	  // 1. Build version history for the initial (pre-conflict) metadata
245	  let versions: VersionEntry[] | undefined;
246	  let prunedCids: string[] = [];
247	
248	  if (params.createVersion) {
249	    const versionEntry: VersionEntry = {
250	      cid: params.currentMetadata.cid,
251	      fileKeyEncrypted: params.currentMetadata.fileKeyEncrypted,
252	      fileIv: params.currentMetadata.fileIv,
253	      size: params.currentMetadata.size,
254	      timestamp: Date.now(),
255	      encryptionMode: params.currentMetadata.encryptionMode ?? 'GCM',
256	    };
257	    const allVersions = [versionEntry, ...(params.currentMetadata.versions ?? [])];
258	
259	    versions = allVersions.slice(0, maxVersions);
260	    prunedCids = allVersions.slice(maxVersions).map((v) => v.cid);
261	  } else {
262	    versions = params.currentMetadata.versions;
263	  }
264	
265	  // 2. Merge updates into current metadata
266	  const updatedMetadata: FileMetadata = {
267	    ...params.currentMetadata,
268	    ...params.updates,
269	    ...(versions && versions.length > 0 ? { versions } : { versions: undefined }),
270	    modifiedAt: Date.now(),
271	  };
272	
273	  // 3+4. Resolve the current IPNS record to establish the CAS base sequence number,
274	  //       then publish with CAS — on 409 apply latest-wins + loser-becomes-version (D-07).
275	  //       The publishWithCas engine owns the resolve→merge→retry skeleton; the
276	  //       domain-specific encode/decode/merge are injected as callbacks.
277	  //       The resolve and "not found" check run INSIDE the try so the fileIpnsPrivateKey
278	  //       is zeroed in the finally on ALL exit paths — including a resolve failure or the
279	  //       early throw (T-47-01 requires caller-owned zeroing on every exit path).
280	  try {
281	    const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(params.fileMetaIpnsName, params.ctx);
282	    if (!resolved) {
283	      throw new Error(
284	        `Cannot update file metadata: existing IPNS record not found for ${params.fileMetaIpnsName}`
285	      );
286	    }
287	
288	    const result = await publishWithCas<FileMetadata>({
289	      ipnsName: params.fileMetaIpnsName,
290	      ipnsPrivateKey: params.fileIpnsPrivateKey,
291	      sequenceNumber: resolved.sequenceNumber,
292	      ctx: params.ctx,
293	      maxAttempts: 4,
294	      backoff: true,
295	      encodeAndUpload: (metadata) => encryptAndUpload(metadata, params.folderKey, params.ctx),
296	      decodeRemote: (cid) => fetchAndDecryptFileMetadata(cid, params.folderKey, params.ctx),
297	      // Latest-wins three-way merge. `base` is unused for files (latest-wins, not
298	      // structural merge); `local` is the writer's metadata, `remote` is the
299	      // conflicting authoritative metadata.
300	      merge: (_base, local, remote) => {
301	        // Latest-wins by modifiedAt (>= prefers local on tie)
302	        const localModifiedAt = local.modifiedAt ?? 0;
303	        const remoteModifiedAt = remote.modifiedAt ?? 0;
304	        const localWins = localModifiedAt >= remoteModifiedAt;
305	
306	        const winner = localWins ? local : remote;
307	        const loser = localWins ? remote : local;
308	
309	        // The loser's current content becomes a VersionEntry
310	        const loserAsVersion: VersionEntry = {
311	          cid: loser.cid,
312	          fileKeyEncrypted: loser.fileKeyEncrypted,
313	          fileIv: loser.fileIv,
314	          size: loser.size,
315	          timestamp: loser.modifiedAt ?? Date.now(),
316	          encryptionMode: (loser.encryptionMode as 'GCM' | 'CTR') ?? 'GCM',
317	        };
318	
319	        // Merge: winner's versions + loserAsVersion merged with the loser's version
320	        // history. The second arg MUST be loser.versions, not remote.versions: when the
321	        // remote wins (localWins === false) the loser is the LOCAL metadata, so passing
322	        // remote.versions would silently drop the local writer's prior version history.
323	        const { versions: mergedVersions, prunedCids: extraPruned } = mergeVersions(
324	          [...(winner.versions ?? []), loserAsVersion],
325	          loser.versions,
326	          maxVersions
327	        );
328	
329	        const mergedMetadata: FileMetadata = {
330	          ...winner,
331	          versions: mergedVersions.length > 0 ? mergedVersions : undefined,
332	          modifiedAt: winner.modifiedAt ?? Date.now(),
333	        };
334	
335	        // CR-02 / D-07: a CID resurrected into mergedMetadata.versions by the remote
336	        // merge must NOT be returned for unpinning. Filter extraPruned against the set
337	        // of CIDs actually referenced by the published mergedMetadata. publishWithCas
338	        // de-dupes the accumulated prunedCids across attempts; this callback only filters
339	        // the CIDs it produces this round.
340	        const referenced = new Set([
341	          mergedMetadata.cid,
342	          ...(mergedMetadata.versions ?? []).map((v) => v.cid),
343	        ]);
344	        const filteredPruned = extraPruned.filter((c) => !referenced.has(c));
345	
346	        return { merged: mergedMetadata, prunedCids: filteredPruned };
347	      },
348	      localData: updatedMetadata,
349	    });
350	
351	    // Combine the pre-conflict prunedCids (from the initial version-history cap) with
352	    // any pruned during conflict merges, then strip CIDs the final published metadata
353	    // still references (CR-02): the initial prune may include a CID that a remote merge
354	    // resurrected into versions.
355	    const finalReferenced = new Set([
356	      result.publishedData.cid,
357	      ...(result.publishedData.versions ?? []).map((v) => v.cid),
358	    ]);
359	    const combinedPruned = [...new Set([...prunedCids, ...result.prunedCids])].filter(
360	      (c) => !finalReferenced.has(c)
361	    );
362	
363	    return {
364	      ipnsName: params.fileMetaIpnsName,
365	      metadataCid: result.cid,
366	      newSequenceNumber: result.newSequenceNumber,
367	      prunedCids: combinedPruned,
368	    };
369	  } finally {
370	    // Zeroize the private key on all exit paths (T-47-01 / T-44-12). publishWithCas
371	    // never zeroes keys; the caller (this function) owns zeroing.
372	    params.fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0);
373	  }
374	}
375	
376	/**
377	 * Helper: encrypt FileMetadata with folderKey and upload to IPFS.
378	 * Returns the resulting CID.
379	 */
380	async function encryptAndUpload(
381	  metadata: FileMetadata,
382	  folderKey: Uint8Array,
383	  ctx: SdkContext
384	): Promise<string> {
385	  const encrypted = await encryptFileMetadata(metadata, folderKey);
386	  const jsonStr = JSON.stringify(encrypted);
387	  const encryptedBytes = new TextEncoder().encode(jsonStr);
388	  const { cid } = await addToIpfs(ctx, encryptedBytes);
389	  return cid;
390	}
391	
392	/**
393	 * Helper: fetch encrypted FileMetadata from IPFS by CID and decrypt with folderKey.
394	 * The read-half companion to encryptAndUpload (mirrors folder's fetchAndDecryptMetadata).
395	 */
396	async function fetchAndDecryptFileMetadata(
397	  cid: string,
398	  folderKey: Uint8Array,
399	  ctx: SdkContext
400	): Promise<FileMetadata> {
401	  const encryptedBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(ctx, cid);
402	  const encryptedJson = new TextDecoder().decode(encryptedBytes);
403	  const encrypted: EncryptedFileMetadata = JSON.parse(encryptedJson);
404	  return decryptFileMetadata(encrypted, folderKey);
405	}
406	
```

The CAS path returns immediately on success and throws `ConflictError` on exhaustion (a reject, not a hang). A 30s `waitFor hidden` timeout means the save promise **rejected** and the modal stayed open. The most valuable evidence is the actual on-screen error. 2	import type { FilePointer } from '@cipherbox/core';

14	type TextEditorDialogProps = {
15	  open: boolean;
16	  onClose: () => void;
17	  item: FilePointer | null;
18	  parentFolderId: string;
19	  /** Parent folder's decrypted AES-256 key (needed to decrypt file metadata) */
20	  folderKey: Uint8Array | null;
21	  /** When true, textarea is read-only and save is hidden */
22	  readOnly?: boolean;
23	  /** Share ID when viewing from a shared folder — uses re-wrapped file keys */
24	  shareId?: string | null;
25	  /** Save handler for shared files — updates file IPNS metadata via shared path */
26	  onSaveSharedFile?: (item: FilePointer, newContent: Uint8Array) => Promise<void>;
27	};
28	
29	/**
30	 * Modal dialog for viewing/editing text files in-browser.
31	 *
32	 * In edit mode (default): full crypto round-trip with save.
33	 * In read-only mode: decrypt and display only (used for shared files).
34	 */
35	export function TextEditorDialog({
36	  open,
37	  onClose,
38	  item,
39	  parentFolderId,
40	  folderKey,
41	  readOnly,
42	  shareId,
43	  onSaveSharedFile,
44	}: TextEditorDialogProps) {
45	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
46	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
47	  const [content, setContent] = useState('');
48	  const [originalContent, setOriginalContent] = useState('');
49	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
50	  const textareaRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
51	
52	  const { updateFile } = useFolder();
53	
54	  const isDirty = content !== originalContent;
55	  const lineCount = content.split('\n').length;
56	
57	  // Load file content when dialog opens
58	  useEffect(() => {
59	    if (!open || !item) {
60	      setContent('');
61	      setOriginalContent('');
62	      setError(null);
63	      setLoading(false);
64	      setSaving(false);
65	      return;
66	    }
67	
68	    if (!folderKey) {
69	      setLoading(false);
70	      setError('Folder key not available');
71	      return;
72	    }
73	
74	    if (!item.fileMetaIpnsName) {
75	      setLoading(false);
76	      setError('File metadata IPNS name not available (legacy v1 file?)');
77	      return;
78	    }
79	
80	    let cancelled = false;
81	    let focusRaf: number | undefined;
82	    setLoading(true);
83	    setError(null);
84	
85	    (async () => {
86	      try {
87	        const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
88	        if (!auth.vaultKeypair) {
89	          throw new Error('No keypair available - please log in again');
90	        }
91	
92	        let plaintext: Uint8Array;
93	
94	        if (shareId) {
95	          // Shared file path: use re-wrapped file key from share_keys
96	          const [{ metadata: fileMeta }, keys] = await Promise.all([
97	            resolveFileMetadata(item.fileMetaIpnsName, folderKey!),
98	            fetchShareKeys(shareId),
99	          ]);
100	
101	          // Exact match by itemId (folder share navigating to a specific file),
102	          // or fallback to first file key (standalone file share where id is shareId)
103	          const fileKeyRecord =
104	            keys.find((k) => k.keyType === 'file' && k.itemId === item.id) ||
105	            keys.find((k) => k.keyType === 'file');
106	          if (!fileKeyRecord) {
107	            throw new Error('File key not available — the folder owner may need to re-share');
108	          }
109	          const wrappedKey = fileKeyRecord.encryptedKey;
110	
111	          plaintext = await downloadFile(
112	            {
113	              cid: fileMeta.cid,
114	              iv: fileMeta.fileIv,
115	              wrappedKey,
116	              originalName: item.name,
117	              encryptionMode: fileMeta.encryptionMode,
118	            },
119	            auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey
120	          );
121	        } else if (hasSdkClient()) {
122	          // Owner path via SDK: resolves IPNS, decrypts metadata, downloads + decrypts content
123	          const client = getSdkClient();
124	          plaintext = await client.downloadFromIpns(item.fileMetaIpnsName, folderKey!);
125	        } else {
126	          throw new Error('SDK not initialized — please log in again');
127	        }
128	
129	        if (cancelled) return;
130	
131	        const text = new TextDecoder().decode(plaintext);
132	        setContent(text);
133	        setOriginalContent(text);
134	        setLoading(false);
135	
136	        // Focus textarea after content loads (only in edit mode)
137	        if (!readOnly) {
138	          focusRaf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
139	            textareaRef.current?.focus();
140	          });
141	        }
142	      } catch (err) {
143	        if (cancelled) return;
144	        setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load file');
145	        setLoading(false);
146	      }
147	    })();
148	
149	    return () => {
150	      cancelled = true;
151	      if (focusRaf !== undefined) cancelAnimationFrame(focusRaf);
152	    };
153	  }, [open, item, folderKey, shareId, readOnly]);
154	
155	  const handleSave = useCallback(async () => {
156	    if (!item || !isDirty) return;
157	
158	    setSaving(true);
159	    setError(null);
160	
161	    try {
162	      if (shareId && onSaveSharedFile) {
163	        // Shared file path: delegate to shared navigation handler
164	        const encoded = new TextEncoder().encode(content);
165	        await onSaveSharedFile(item, encoded);
166	      } else {
167	        // Owner path: encrypt and update folder metadata
168	        const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
169	        if (!auth.vaultKeypair) {
170	          throw new Error('No keypair available - please log in again');
171	        }
172	
173	        const encoded = new TextEncoder().encode(content);
174	        const file = new File([encoded], item.name);
175	        const encrypted = await encryptFile(file, auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey);
176	        const blob = new Blob([encrypted.ciphertext as BlobPart]);
177	        const { cid } = await addToIpfs(blob);
178	
179	        await updateFile(parentFolderId, {
180	          fileId: item.id,
181	          newCid: cid,
182	          newFileKeyEncrypted: encrypted.wrappedKey,
183	          newFileIv: encrypted.iv,
184	          newSize: encrypted.originalSize,
185	        });
186	      }
187	
188	      onClose();
189	    } catch (err) {
190	      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to save file');
191	      setSaving(false);
192	    }
193	  }, [item, content, isDirty, parentFolderId, updateFile, onClose, shareId, onSaveSharedFile]);
194	
195	  // Handle Ctrl/Cmd+S to save (edit mode only)
196	  useEffect(() => {
197	    if (!open || readOnly) return;
198	
199	    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
200	      if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key === 's') {
201	        e.preventDefault();
202	        if (isDirty && !saving && !loading) {
203	          handleSave();
204	        }
205	      }
206	    };
207	
208	    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
209	    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
210	  }, [open, readOnly, isDirty, saving, loading, handleSave]);
211	
212	  const handleDownload = useCallback(() => {
213	    if (!item || !content) return;
214	    const encoded = new TextEncoder().encode(content);
215	    triggerBrowserDownload(encoded, item.name, 'text/plain');
216	  }, [item, content]);
217	
218	  if (!item) return null;
219	
220	  const title = readOnly ? `View: ${item.name}` : `Edit: ${item.name}`;
221	  const isDisabled = saving || loading;
222	
223	  return (
224	    <Modal
225	      open={open}
226	      onClose={isDisabled ? undefined : onClose}
227	      title={title}
228	      className="text-editor-modal"
229	    >
230	      {loading ? (
231	        <div className="text-editor-loading">decrypting...</div>
232	      ) : (
233	        <div className="text-editor-body">
234	          <textarea
235	            ref={textareaRef}
236	            className="text-editor-textarea"
237	            value={content}
238	            onChange={readOnly ? undefined : (e) => setContent(e.target.value)}
239	            disabled={isDisabled}
240	            readOnly={readOnly}
241	            spellCheck={false}
242	            aria-label={`Content of ${item.name}`}
243	          />
244	          <div className={`text-editor-status${isDirty ? ' text-editor-status--modified' : ''}`}>
245	            <span>
246	              {'// '}
247	              {lineCount} {lineCount === 1 ? 'line' : 'lines'}
248	              {' | utf-8'}
249	              {readOnly ? ' | read-only' : ''}
250	              {isDirty ? ' | modified' : ''}
251	            </span>
252	          </div>
253	          {error && (
254	            <div className="text-editor-error">
255	              {'> '}
256	              {error}
257	            </div>
258	          )}
259	          <div className="text-editor-footer">
260	            {readOnly ? (
261	              <>
262	                <button
263	                  type="button"
264	                  className="dialog-button dialog-button--secondary"
265	                  onClick={handleDownload}
266	                >
267	                  --download
268	                </button>
269	                <button
270	                  type="button"
271	                  className="dialog-button dialog-button--primary"
272	                  onClick={onClose}
273	                >
274	                  close
275	                </button>
276	              </>
277	            ) : (
278	              <>
279	                <button
280	                  type="button"
281	                  className="dialog-button dialog-button--secondary"
282	                  onClick={onClose}
283	                  disabled={isDisabled}
284	                >
285	                  cancel
286	                </button>
287	                <button
288	                  type="button"
289	                  className="dialog-button dialog-button--primary"
290	                  onClick={handleSave}
291	                  disabled={isDisabled || !isDirty}
292	                >
293	                  {saving ? 'encrypting...' : '--save'}
294	                </button>
295	              </>
296	            )}
297	          </div>
298	        </div>
299	      )}
300	    </Modal>
301	  );
302	}

1	import { useCallback } from 'react';
2	import { useFolderStore } from '../stores/folder.store';

5	import { useFileOperations } from './useFileOperations';

8	/**
9	 * React hook for folder operations with loading/error state.
10	 *
11	 * Composes focused sub-hooks for folder mutations, file operations,
12	 * and file version management into a single unified API.
13	 *
14	 * @example
15	 * ```tsx
16	 * function FolderActions() {
17	 *   const { isLoading, error, createFolder, deleteItem } = useFolder();
18	 *
19	 *   const handleCreate = async () => {
20	 *     try {
21	 *       await createFolder('New Folder', currentFolderId);
22	 *     } catch (err) {
23	 *       // Error also in error state
24	 *     }
25	 *   };
26	 *
27	 *   return (
28	 *     <div>
29	 *       {isLoading && <Spinner />}
30	 *       {error && <ErrorMessage>{error}</ErrorMessage>}
31	 *       <button onClick={handleCreate}>New Folder</button>
32	 *     </div>
33	 *   );
34	 * }
35	 * ```
36	 */
37	export function useFolder() {
38	  const folderMutations = useFolderMutations();
39	  const fileOperations = useFileOperations();
40	  const fileVersions = useFileVersions();
41	  const folderStore = useFolderStore();
42	
43	  // Combine loading/error state from all sub-hooks
44	  const isLoading = folderMutations.isLoading || fileOperations.isLoading || fileVersions.isLoading;
45	  const error = folderMutations.error || fileOperations.error || fileVersions.error;
46	
47	  const clearError = useCallback(() => {
48	    // This is intentionally simplified - errors clear when the next operation starts
49	  }, []);
50	
51	  return {
52	    isLoading,
53	    error,
54	    createFolder: folderMutations.createFolder,
55	    renameItem: folderMutations.renameItem,
56	    moveItem: folderMutations.moveItem,
57	    moveItems: folderMutations.moveItems,
58	    deleteItem: folderMutations.deleteItem,
59	    deleteItems: folderMutations.deleteItems,
60	    updateFile: fileOperations.updateFile,
61	    restoreVersion: fileVersions.restoreVersion,
62	    deleteVersion: fileVersions.deleteVersion,
63	    clearError,
64	    calculateFolderDepth: (folderId: string) => calculateFolderDepth(folderId, folderStore.folders),
65	  };
66	}

1	import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
2	import { useFolderStore } from '../stores/folder.store';
3	import { useVaultStore } from '../stores/vault.store';
4	import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth.store';
5	import { useQuotaStore } from '../stores/quota.store';
6	import { unpinFromIpfs } from '../lib/api/ipfs';
7	import { getSdkClient } from '../lib/sdk-provider';
8	import { reWrapForRecipients } from '../services/share.service';
9	import {
10	  resolveFileMetadata,
11	  shouldCreateVersion,
12	  getFileIpnsPrivateKey,
13	} from '../services/file-metadata.service';
14	import type { FilePointer } from '@cipherbox/core';
15	import { unwrapKey, hexToBytes } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
16	import { getRootFolderState } from './folder-helpers';
17	import type { FolderOperationState } from './folder-helpers';
18	import { useVaultSettingsStore } from '../stores/vault-settings.store';
19	import { logger } from '../lib/logger';
20	
21	/**
22	 * React hook for file add/update operations.
23	 *
24	 * Returns loading/error state and operation callbacks.
25	 */
26	export function useFileOperations() {
27	  const [state, setState] = useState<FolderOperationState>({
28	    isLoading: false,
29	    error: null,
30	  });
31	
32	  /**
33	   * Update a file's content in-place (v2: re-encrypt, update file IPNS, folder untouched).
34	   *
35	   * Old CIDs are preserved as version history (VER-01). Only pruned versions
36	   * (excess beyond max 10) have their CIDs unpinned.
37	   *
38	   * NOTE: No conflict detection here -- handleUpdateFile publishes only the
39	   * per-file IPNS record. Folder metadata is NOT touched, so no 409 is possible.
40	   *
41	   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID ('root' or folder UUID)
42	   * @param fileData - New file data after re-encryption
43	   * @returns Resolves when the update is complete
44	   */
45	  const handleUpdateFile = useCallback(
46	    async (
47	      parentId: string,
48	      fileData: {
49	        fileId: string;
50	        newCid: string;
51	        newFileKeyEncrypted: string;
52	        newFileIv: string;
53	        newSize: number;
54	        newEncryptionMode?: 'GCM' | 'CTR';
55	        forceVersion?: boolean; // true for web re-upload, false/undefined for text editor
56	      }
57	    ): Promise<void> => {
58	      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
59	      try {
60	        const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
61	        const vault = useVaultStore.getState();
62	        const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
63	
64	        if (!auth.vaultKeypair) {
65	          throw new Error('No ECIES keypair available - please log in again');
66	        }
67	
68	        // Get parent folder state
69	        const parentFolder =
70	          parentId === 'root' ? getRootFolderState(vault, folders) : folders[parentId];
71	
72	        if (!parentFolder) {
73	          throw new Error('Parent folder not found or vault not initialized');
74	        }
75	
76	        // 1. Find the FilePointer in parent's children
77	        const filePointer = parentFolder.children.find(
78	          (c) => c.type === 'file' && c.id === fileData.fileId
79	        ) as FilePointer | undefined;
80	
81	        if (!filePointer) {
82	          throw new Error('File not found in folder');
83	        }
84	
85	        // 2. Resolve current file metadata from IPNS
86	        const { metadata: currentMetadata } = await resolveFileMetadata(
87	          filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
88	          parentFolder.folderKey
89	        );
90	
91	        // 3. Decrypt the file IPNS private key from FilePointer (or HKDF fallback)
92	        const { privateKey: fileIpnsPrivateKey, migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted } =
93	          await getFileIpnsPrivateKey(
94	            filePointer,
95	            auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
96	            auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey
97	          );
98	
99	        // 4. Determine whether to create a version entry
100	        const createVersion = shouldCreateVersion(currentMetadata, fileData.forceVersion ?? false);
101	
102	        let prunedCids: string[];
103	        try {
104	          // 5/6. Route the file replace through the SDK client. client.replaceFile owns
105	          //    the file IPNS publish (via sdk-core updateFileMetadata, CAS internally),
106	          //    the folder touch (bump modifiedAt on the FilePointer + persist any migrated
107	          //    IPNS key), folderTree bookkeeping, and the folder:updated emission. The
108	          //    store's children/sequenceNumber are written ONLY by the folder:updated
109	          //    subscription — no direct store writes here (PR #489 desync closed).
110	          //    fileIpnsPrivateKey is resolved above and zeroed in this finally (T-47-01);
111	          //    sdk-core updateFileMetadata also zeroes its own copy.
112	          ({ prunedCids } = await getSdkClient().replaceFile(
113	            parentFolder.ipnsName,
114	            fileData.fileId,
115	            {
116	              fileIpnsPrivateKey,
117	              currentMetadata,
118	              updates: {
119	                cid: fileData.newCid,
120	                fileKeyEncrypted: fileData.newFileKeyEncrypted,
121	                fileIv: fileData.newFileIv,
122	                size: fileData.newSize,
123	                ...(fileData.newEncryptionMode
124	                  ? { encryptionMode: fileData.newEncryptionMode }
125	                  : {}),
126	              },
127	              createVersion,
128	              maxVersionsPerFile: useVaultSettingsStore.getState().settings.maxVersionsPerFile,
129	              ...(migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted ? { migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted } : {}),
130	            }
131	          ));
132	        } finally {
133	          fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0);
134	        }
135	
136	        // 8. Re-wrap new file key for share recipients (fire-and-forget)
137	        (async () => {
138	          try {
139	            const authState = useAuthStore.getState();
140	            if (!authState.vaultKeypair) return;
141	            const fileKey = await unwrapKey(
142	              hexToBytes(fileData.newFileKeyEncrypted),
143	              authState.vaultKeypair.privateKey
144	            );
145	            try {
146	              await reWrapForRecipients({
147	                folderIpnsName: parentFolder.ipnsName,
148	                folders: useFolderStore.getState().folders,
149	                currentFolderId: parentId === 'root' ? null : parentId,
150	                newItems: [{ keyType: 'file', itemId: fileData.fileId, plaintextKey: fileKey }],
151	              });
152	            } finally {
153	              fileKey.fill(0);
154	            }
155	          } catch (err) {
156	            logger.warn('[share] Post-update re-wrap failed:', err);
157	          }
158	        })();
159	
160	        // 9. Only unpin CIDs of pruned versions (excess beyond max 10)
161	        // Old CIDs stay pinned as version history (VER-01)
162	        for (const prunedCid of prunedCids) {
163	          unpinFromIpfs(prunedCid).catch((err) =>
164	            logger.warn('[FileOps] Unpin pruned CID failed:', err)
165	          );
166	        }
167	
168	        // Refresh quota
169	        useQuotaStore.getState().fetchQuota();
170	
171	        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
172	      } catch (err) {
173	        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to update file';
174	        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
175	        throw err;
176	      }
177	    },
178	    []
179	  );
180	
181	  return {
182	    ...state,
183	    updateFile: handleUpdateFile,
184	  };
185	}
186

The owner path now delegates to `getSdkClient().replaceFile(...)`. The critical change is in the comment at line 104-109: the **OLD** owner file-edit did **not** touch folder metadata ("Folder metadata is NOT touched, so no 409 is possible" — line 38-39 stale comment), but the **NEW** `client.replaceFile` does a **folder touch** (bump `modifiedAt` + persist migrated IPNS key). That introduces a folder-IPNS publish — and a 409 path — that didn't exist before. Let me read `client.replaceFile`.

> AGENT

`replaceFile` step 3 (lines 1183-1207) is the smoking gun: an **unconditional folder touch** — bump `modifiedAt` and publish folder metadata with `sequenceNumber: folder.sequenceNumber` from `folderTree`. The OLD owner edit path never touched the folder. Two failure modes here: `folderTree.get(parentIpnsName)` returns undefined → "Folder not loaded" throw, or a stale `folder.sequenceNumber` → folder-IPNS 409. Let me check the CI log for evidence of which.

> AGENT

I've confirmed the diagnosis and have a strong lead; the verification workflow is finishing the pinpoint. Here's where it stands:

## Confirmed so far

**This is a deterministic regression from PR #494 (`d17d42e5f`), not a flake.** Proof:
- Parent commit `0c2422c8c` (#495) ran the full E2E workflow and **passed Web E2E twice** (16:04, 16:28).
- Its child `d17d42e5f` (#494, the SDK folder-state/publish consolidation) **failed Web E2E** on the one test that exercises the changed save path.
- The only diff between them is #494.

**Why it slipped through:** as you said, Web E2E doesn't run on PRs — only `SDK E2E` + the `Verify E2E Passed` gate ran on #494 (and that gate passes when Web E2E is skipped). Web E2E only runs on push to `main`, so the regression was first observed post-merge.

**The failure:** `writable-shares.spec.ts:330` — Alice (the folder *owner*) edits a file Bob uploaded into her writably-shared folder, presses Ctrl+S, and the editor modal never closes (30s timeout). The modal only closes on a *successful* save, so the save promise **rejected**.

**Leading mechanism:** the owner save now routes through `client.replaceFile` (`packages/sdk/src/client.ts:1145`), whose **step 3 does an unconditional folder "touch"** — bump `modifiedAt` and `updateFolderMetadataAndPublish` using `folder.sequenceNumber` from `folderTree`. The **old** owner edit path explicitly never touched the folder (`useFileOperations.ts:38` — *"Folder metadata is NOT touched, so no 409 is possible"*). That new folder publish (or a `folderTree`-not-loaded throw) is the regression surface.

The 3-reader + 3-verifier workflow is reading the actual #494 diff to confirm whether it's the folder-touch 409/stale-sequence path vs a `folderTree.get()` "Folder not loaded" throw, and to nail the minimal fix. > AGENT

Unanimous consensus — all 3 readers and all 3 adversarial verifiers (every lens `isReal: true`, high confidence). Root cause confirmed:

**`client.replaceFile` (added by #494, `client.ts:1161`) gates on `folderTree.get(parentIpnsName)` and throws `'Folder not loaded'` if absent. The web app only populates `folderTree` via `ensureFolderRegistered` — called from upload/mutation hooks, never from navigation/read or the editor-save path. Alice's `page.reload()` in 4.1 wipes the client's folderTree; 4.1/4.2 are read-only; so at 4.3 (first owner write post-reload) the folder is missing → synchronous throw → save rejects → modal never closes → 30s timeout.**

Let me verify the fix surface before editing: `ensureFolderRegistered`'s signature, and the parallel gap in `useFileVersions.ts`.

1	/**
2	 * SDK Provider - CipherBoxClient lifecycle management
3	 *
4	 * Manages a singleton CipherBoxClient instance for the web app.
5	 * Created after vault load (login complete), destroyed on logout.
6	 *
7	 * The SDK client is the single source of truth for all file operations.
8	 * Hooks call client methods, and stores subscribe to client events.
9	 */
10	import { CipherBoxClient, type CipherBoxClientConfig } from '@cipherbox/sdk';
11	import type { PinningConfig } from '@cipherbox/sdk';
12	import { apiAxios } from './api-config';
13	import { destroyEncryptionWorker } from '../services/encrypt-worker.service';
14	import type { FolderNode } from '../stores/folder.store';
15	
16	let _client: CipherBoxClient | null = null;
17	let _lastConfig: CipherBoxClientConfig | null = null;
18	
19	/**
20	 * Initialize the SDK client. Called after vault is loaded (login complete).
21	 * The client persists for the session and is destroyed on logout.
22	 *
23	 * If a client already exists (e.g., session restoration), it is destroyed
24	 * before creating the new one.
25	 */
26	export function initSdkClient(config: CipherBoxClientConfig): CipherBoxClient {
27	  if (_client) {
28	    _client.destroy();
29	  }
30	  // Inject the shared axios instance so CipherBoxClient uses the same
31	  // instance as orval-generated functions (single instance, no dual path).
32	  _lastConfig = { ...config };
33	  _client = new CipherBoxClient({ ...config, axiosInstance: apiAxios });
34	  return _client;
35	}
36	
37	/**
38	 * Get the current SDK client instance.
39	 * Throws if called before initSdkClient() (i.e., before login).
40	 */
41	export function getSdkClient(): CipherBoxClient {
42	  if (!_client) {
43	    throw new Error('SDK client not initialized. Call initSdkClient() after login.');
44	  }
45	  return _client;
46	}
47	
48	/**
49	 * Check if the SDK client is initialized (for conditional usage).
50	 */
51	export function hasSdkClient(): boolean {
52	  return _client !== null;
53	}
54	
55	/**
56	 * Destroy the SDK client. Called on logout.
57	 * Clears internal state, key caches, and event subscriptions.
58	 */
59	export function destroySdkClient(): void {
60	  if (_client) {
61	    _client.destroy();
62	    _client = null;
63	  }
64	  _lastConfig = null;
65	  // Terminate encryption Web Worker (no-op if not initialized)
66	  destroyEncryptionWorker();
67	}
68	
69	/**
70	 * Reconfigure the SDK client's pinning config at runtime.
71	 * Called from StorageTab after saving new BYO settings.
72	 *
73	 * Destroys the current client and recreates it with updated pinningConfig
74	 * while preserving all other configuration. This is acceptable since config
75	 * changes are infrequent (only on Settings save).
76	 */
77	export function reconfigurePinning(pinningConfig?: PinningConfig): void {
78	  if (!_client || !_lastConfig) return;
79	  _client.destroy();
80	  _lastConfig = { ..._lastConfig, pinningConfig };
81	  _client = new CipherBoxClient({ ..._lastConfig, axiosInstance: apiAxios });
82	}
83	
84	/**
85	 * Ensure a folder from the Zustand store is registered in the SDK's
86	 * internal FolderTree. This bridges the gap between folder navigation
87	 * (which loads folders into Zustand) and SDK operations (which require
88	 * folders in the SDK's internal state).
89	 *
90	 * Registers the folder if the SDK doesn't already track it. If it does, the
91	 * SDK's internal folderTree is authoritative and left untouched: all
92	 * folder-state mutations (including file replace/version edits) now route
93	 * through SDK client methods, so the SDK sequence never lags the store and no
94	 * forward reconciliation is needed (Phase 47 / PR #489 closure).
95	 */
96	export function ensureFolderRegistered(folder: FolderNode): void {
97	  const client = getSdkClient();
98	
99	  // Already tracked: the SDK folderTree is authoritative. No reconciliation —
100	  // every folder-state mutation flows through the client, so the store can no
101	  // longer advance past the SDK's internal sequence.
102	  if (client.hasFolder(folder.ipnsName)) {
103	    return;
104	  }
105	
106	  // Guard: don't register placeholder folders with empty key material —
107	  // would cause crypto/IPNS errors on subsequent mutations
108	  if (folder.folderKey.length === 0 || folder.ipnsPrivateKey.length === 0) {
109	    return;
110	  }
111	
112	  client.registerFolder(
113	    folder.ipnsName,
114	    folder.folderKey,
115	    {
116	      publicKey: new Uint8Array(0), // Public key derived from private key when needed
117	      privateKey: folder.ipnsPrivateKey,
118	    },
119	    folder.children,
120	    folder.sequenceNumber
121	  );
122	}
123	
```

`ensureFolderRegistered(folder: FolderNode)` is exactly the bridge — registers the store folder into `folderTree` if not already tracked, no-op otherwise. The fix is to call it before routing through the client, matching the existing mutation-hook pattern. > AGENT

Done — here's the summary while the E2E verifies on the branch.

## Root cause (deterministic #494 regression, unanimously verified)

PR #494 re-routed three web write paths through new `CipherBoxClient` methods — `replaceFile` (owner edit), `restoreFileVersion`, `deleteFileVersion`. Each starts with:

```ts
const folder = this.folderTree.get(parentIpnsName);
if (!folder) throw new Error('Folder not loaded');
```

The web app seeds `folderTree` **only** via `ensureFolderRegistered`, which #494 wired into the upload/mutation hooks but **missed in these three call sites**. Navigation/read populate only the Zustand store, never `folderTree`. In test 4.3, Alice's `page.reload()` (in 4.1) recreates the client with an empty `folderTree`; 4.1/4.2 are read-only; so 4.3 — her first owner write post-reload — throws synchronously. The rejection leaves the editor modal open → the 30s `waitFor(hidden)` timeout.

Why it slipped through: **Web E2E doesn't run on PRs** (as you noted) — only on push to `main`. I confirmed it: parent commit `0c2422c8c` passed Web E2E twice; `d17d42e5f` (#494) failed it.

## Fix

Added `ensureFolderRegistered(parentFolder)` before the client call in all three sites (`useFileOperations.ts` + both paths in `useFileVersions.ts`), mirroring `useFolderMutations`/`useDropUpload`. No-op when already tracked. `tsc --noEmit` and `eslint` both green.

## Status

- Branch `fix/owner-edit-folder-not-loaded` → commit `fc05bf87b`, pushed.
- CI E2E dispatched on the branch (forces web+desktop): **https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/actions/runs/27579074392**
- I'm polling the **Web E2E** job in the background and will report pass/fail when it lands (~10 min). Once green, this is ready to open as a PR.

1	import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
2	import { useBinStore } from '../stores/bin.store';
3	import { useVaultSettingsStore } from '../stores/vault-settings.store';
4	import { getSdkClient, hasSdkClient } from '../lib/sdk-provider';
5	import type { BinEntry } from '@cipherbox/core';
6	import { logger } from '../lib/logger';
7	
8	/**
9	 * React hook for recycle bin operations.
10	 *
11	 * Delegates to CipherBoxClient SDK methods for bin CRUD operations.
12	 * The SDK handles bin metadata, IPNS publishing, and event emission.
13	 * Loading/error state and purge logic remain in the hook.
14	 */
15	export function useBin() {
16	  const [state, setState] = useState<{ isLoading: boolean; error: string | null }>({
17	    isLoading: false,
18	    error: null,
19	  });
20	
21	  const entries = useBinStore((s) => s.entries);
22	  const isLoaded = useBinStore((s) => s.isLoaded);
23	  const retentionDays = useVaultSettingsStore((s) => s.settings.recycleBinRetentionDays);
24	
25	  /**
26	   * Load bin metadata from IPNS and trigger auto-purge of expired entries.
27	   *
28	   * Uses CipherBoxClient.loadBin() to load bin from IPNS and populate
29	   * the SDK bin state. The SDK emits bin:updated events which the store
30	   * subscription handles.
31	   */
32	  const loadBin = useCallback(async () => {
33	    if (!hasSdkClient()) return;
34	
35	    setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
36	    try {
37	      // Load bin via SDK client (handles IPNS resolve, decrypt, auto-repair)
38	      const binState = await getSdkClient().loadBin();
39	
40	      // Update Zustand store from SDK-returned state
41	      const binStore = useBinStore.getState();
42	      binStore.setBinIpnsName(binState.ipnsName);
43	      binStore.setEntries(binState.entries, binState.sequenceNumber);
44	
45	      // Non-blocking: purge expired entries after loading
46	      // Use vault settings store as single source of truth for retention
47	      const currentRetention = useVaultSettingsStore.getState().settings.recycleBinRetentionDays;
48	      void getSdkClient()
49	        .purgeExpired(currentRetention)
50	        .catch(() => {
51	          logger.error('[useBin] Auto-purge failed (non-blocking)');
52	        });
53	
54	      setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
55	    } catch (err) {
56	      const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load bin';
57	      setState({ isLoading: false, error });
58	    }
59	  }, []);
60	
61	  /**
62	   * Restore a single bin entry to its original folder via SDK.
63	   */
64	  const restore = useCallback(async (entryId: string) => {
65	    setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
66	    try {
67	      // Look up the target folder from the bin entry's metadata
68	      const binEntries = useBinStore.getState().entries;
69	      const entry = binEntries.find((e) => e.id === entryId);
70	      if (!entry) throw new Error('Bin entry not found');
71	
72	      const client = getSdkClient();
73	      await client.restoreFromBin(entryId, entry.originalParentIpnsName);
74	      // SDK emits bin:updated -> store subscription updates entries

295	/**
296	 * Restore an item from the recycle bin back to a folder.
297	 *
298	 * Removes the entry from bin metadata, adds the preserved FolderChild
299	 * back to the target folder's children, and publishes both.
300	 */
301	export async function restoreFromBin(params: {
302	  entryId: string;
303	  targetFolderIpnsName: string;
304	  folderTree: FolderTree;
305	  binState: BinState;
306	  binCtx: BinOperationContext;
307	}): Promise<{ restoredItem: FolderChild; updatedBinState: BinState }> {
308	  // 1. Find the bin entry
309	  const entry = params.binState.entries.find((e) => e.id === params.entryId);
310	  if (!entry) throw new Error('Bin entry not found');
311	
312	  // 2. Get the FolderChild to restore
313	  let child: FolderChild;
314	  if (entry.itemType === 'file' && entry.filePointer) {
315	    child = { ...entry.filePointer, modifiedAt: Date.now() };
316	  } else if (entry.itemType === 'folder' && entry.folderEntry) {
317	    child = { ...entry.folderEntry, modifiedAt: Date.now() };
318	  } else {
319	    throw new Error('Bin entry has no stored item reference');
320	  }
321	
322	  // 3. Add child back to target folder
323	  const targetFolder = params.folderTree.get(params.targetFolderIpnsName);
324	  if (!targetFolder) throw new Error('Target folder not loaded');
325	
326	  // Handle name collisions
327	  const existingNames = new Set(targetFolder.children.map((c) => c.name));
328	  if (existingNames.has(child.name)) {
329	    let newName = `${child.name} (restored)`;
330	    let counter = 2;
331	    while (existingNames.has(newName)) {
332	      newName = `${child.name} (restored ${counter})`;
333	      counter++;
334	    }
335	    child = { ...child, name: newName };
336	  }
337	
338	  const baseChildren = [...targetFolder.children];
339	  const updatedFolderChildren = [...targetFolder.children, child];
340	
341	  // 4. Publish updated folder metadata
342	  const { newSequenceNumber, publishedChildren } = await sdkCore.updateFolderMetadataAndPublish({
343	    children: updatedFolderChildren,
344	    baseChildren,
345	    folderKey: targetFolder.folderKey,
346	    ipnsPrivateKey: targetFolder.ipnsKeypair.privateKey,
347	    ipnsName: params.targetFolderIpnsName,
348	    sequenceNumber: targetFolder.sequenceNumber,
349	    ctx: params.binCtx.ctx,
350	  });
351	
352	  // 5. Update folder state — adopt merged published set (CR-01)
353	  targetFolder.children = publishedChildren;
354	  targetFolder.sequenceNumber = newSequenceNumber;
355	  targetFolder.lastLoadedAt = Date.now();
356	  params.folderTree.set(params.targetFolderIpnsName, targetFolder);
357	
358	  // 6. Remove entry from bin and publish
359	  const remainingEntries = params.binState.entries.filter((e) => e.id !== params.entryId);
360	  const newBinSeq = params.binState.sequenceNumber + 1;
361	
362	  const metadata: RecycleBinMetadata = {
363	    version: BIN_METADATA_VERSION,
364	    sequenceNumber: newBinSeq,
365	    entries: remainingEntries,
366	  };
367

369

371	    entries: remainingEntries,
372	    sequenceNumber: newBinSeq,
373	    ipnsName: params.binState.ipnsName,
374	  };
375

`restoreFromBin` (`bin/index.ts:323`) genuinely needs the target folder's keys (`folderKey`, `ipnsKeypair`, `children`, `sequenceNumber`) to republish the parent — so it must be in `folderTree`. The simple store-lookup+register fixes the common case but not "reload → open bin → restore without ever navigating to the parent."
