> AGENT

Web E2E is still running (~9 min in, should land shortly — I'll report when the background poll finishes). 

Here's the comb-through result.

## Audit result: clean except one pre-existing gap

The workflow audited **21 call sites** across all 12 folderTree-dependent client methods. Everything is correctly guarded **except bin restore**:

- ✅ **Mutations** (`createFolder`, `renameItem`, `moveItem` ×2, `deleteItem`, `deleteToBin`) — all guarded in `useFolderMutations`.
- ✅ **Uploads** — `useDropUpload` guarded; the `encrypt-worker.service.ts:102` "uploadFiles" is only a JSDoc reference, not a call.
- ✅ **Share** — `client.shareFolder`/`revokeShare`/`reWrapForRecipients` have **zero web callers** (web sharing goes through the REST API, not folderTree).
- ✅ **Shared-write** (`useSharedWriteOps`) — uses standalone SDK functions on a ref-built context, never `folderTree`.
- ✅ Bin `permanentDelete`/`emptyBin` — operate on bin records, not folderTree.
- ❌ **`restoreFromBin`** (single `useBin.ts:73` + batch `:128`) — same class: `bin/index.ts:324` throws `'Target folder not loaded'` if the target parent isn't in `folderTree`, and `useBin` never registers it.

## But it's pre-existing and lower severity

- The folderTree dependency dates to **PR #296**, not #494 — out of this regression's blast radius.
- `recycle-bin.spec.ts` E2E **passes** because it navigates/uploads first (parent already registered) and never reloads.
- The complete fix is **non-trivial**: bin entries only carry `originalParentIpnsName`, the store is keyed by folder `id`, and after a reload the parent may not be in the store at all — so a full fix needs to *resolve+load the parent folder by ipnsName*, not just register a store node. (Root-deleted items are easy since root is always derivable; subfolder-deleted items are the hard case.)

This is a genuine scope decision, so:

The bin entry carries `originalParentIpnsName` + `originalPath` breadcrumb, but **not** the parent's keys — and navigation only populates the Zustand store (via `setFolder`), not `folderTree`. So the full fix needs to load the parent's key chain. Let me read the navigation load logic to find the reusable mechanism.

> AGENT

I've now traced the full design, and it surfaced something material you should weigh before I build it.

## The "full fix" is a from-root tree resolver, not a local guard

To restore an item, `restoreFromBin` must republish the **parent folder**, which needs that folder's `folderKey` + `ipnsPrivateKey`. Those are wrapped inside the parent's `FolderEntry`, which lives in *its* parent — so loading the parent by `ipnsName` from a cold store requires **walking the tree from root**, unwrapping keys at each level. Two hard constraints I confirmed:

- The SDK client **can't self-seed**: its config has `rootIpnsName`/`rootFolderKey`/`vaultKeypair` but **not** the root IPNS *private* key (that lives only in the web `vaultStore`). So the walk must be seeded with root from the web side.
- A correct resolver is a **DFS/BFS over the folder tree by ipnsName** (the `originalPath` breadcrumb is display names — too fragile to walk). Worst case it loads much of the tree.

So the complete fix is roughly: a new SDK `ensureFolderLoaded(targetIpnsName)` walker (~80–120 lines, crypto key-unwrapping in a loop) + web seeding root in `useBin` + a unit test + a new `reload → restore-subfolder-item` E2E + a security-review pass.

## Why I'm pausing

This is a **pre-existing bug (PR #296), not part of the #494 regression** — and it's a sizable, security-sensitive feature. The current branch has a **clean, E2E-verified #494 hotfix that fixes broken `main`**. Bundling a 100+-line crypto resolver into it would delay that hotfix and mix concerns.

**My recommendation:** open the `#494` hotfix PR now (it's verified green), and land the bin resolver as its **own follow-up PR** — I'll start building it immediately either way. This keeps the broken-main fix unblocked and gives the resolver its own focused review + E2E.

> AGENT

You've hit the real architectural root cause — let me address that, then capture it properly.

## Your instinct is correct

`CipherBoxClient` is positioned as the **stateful owner** of all folder operations (it holds `folderTree`, `binState`, `keyCache`, `vaultKeypair`, `rootFolderKey`). But its config (`packages/sdk/src/types.ts`) carries `rootIpnsName` + `rootFolderKey` + `vaultKeypair` and **not** the root IPNS *private* key. So the client literally cannot resolve/publish root or bootstrap its own folder tree — it depends on the web app to feed folders in via `ensureFolderRegistered`.

That asymmetry is the **actual root cause of the entire `'Folder not loaded'` bug class**:
- A truly stateful client could lazy-load any folder from root on demand → no `ensureFolderRegistered` calls needed anywhere.
- Instead, every folderTree-dependent method assumes the web pre-seeded it, and #494's regression was just *three call sites* where that seeding got dropped. Bin restore is a fourth. We're patching symptoms.

The fix you're pointing at: **put `rootIpnsKeypair` in the client config and let the client self-bootstrap + lazy-load** (`ensureFolderLoaded(ipnsName)` DFS-from-root, called internally by the folderTree-dependent methods). That dissolves the whole workaround class, and bin restore falls out for free.

**Security note:** this adds *no* new exposure — the client already holds `vaultKeypair.privateKey`, `rootFolderKey`, and every loaded folder's `ipnsPrivateKey` in memory (zeroed on `destroy()`). The root IPNS private key is strictly *more* consistent there than split across the web `vaultStore`.

Let me capture this as a todo (symptom + the architectural fix):

```json
{
  "skill": "gsd:capture",
  "args": "Bug + architectural fix: SDK client lacks the root IPNS private key, so it can't self-bootstrap/lazy-load the folder tree — the root cause of the whole \"Folder not loaded\" bug class.\n\nSYMPTOM (concrete bug): Restoring a recycle-bin item after a client re-creation (page reload / re-login) throws \"Target folder not loaded\" when the item's original parent folder hasn't been navigated to that session. Throw site: packages/sdk/src/bin/index.ts:324 (restoreFromBin derefs folderTree.get(targetFolderIpnsName)). Web callers apps/web/src/hooks/useBin.ts:73 (restore) and :128 (restoreMultiple) never call ensureFolderRegistered. Pre-existing since PR #296 (not a #494 regression). Not caught by tests/web-e2e/tests/recycle-bin.spec.ts because that test navigates/uploads first (parent already in folderTree) and never reloads.\n\nROOT CAUSE (architectural): CipherBoxClient is the stateful owner of folder operations but CipherBoxClientConfig (packages/sdk/src/types.ts) carries rootIpnsName + rootFolderKey + vaultKeypair and NOT the root IPNS private key (rootIpnsKeypair). The root ipns private key lives only in the web vaultStore. So the client cannot resolve/publish root or lazy-load folders from root on its own; the web app must seed folderTree via ensureFolderRegistered before every folderTree-dependent client method (replaceFile, restoreFileVersion, deleteFileVersion, restoreFromBin, renameItem, deleteItem, uploadFile(s), moveItem, createFolder, shareFolder). The #494 regression (fixed on branch fix/owner-edit-folder-not-loaded) was simply three of those seeding calls being dropped; bin restore is a fourth missing one. We are patching symptoms.\n\nRECOMMENDED FIX: (1) Add rootIpnsKeypair to CipherBoxClientConfig and have the client self-bootstrap (resolve+register root on init or lazily). (2) Implement an internal lazy ensureFolderLoaded(targetIpnsName) that DFS-walks from root, unwrapping each FolderEntry's keys with the vault keypair and loading metadata until the target is registered. (3) Call it inside all folderTree-dependent methods (definitely restoreFromBin; also as a safety net for replaceFile/restoreFileVersion/deleteFileVersion/rename/delete/upload/move). This dissolves the web-side ensureFolderRegistered workaround entirely and closes the whole \"Folder not loaded\" class.\n\nSECURITY: no new exposure — the client already holds vaultKeypair.privateKey, rootFolderKey, and every loaded folder's ipnsPrivateKey in memory (zeroed on destroy()). Root ipns private key is consistent with that.\n\nTESTS NEEDED: SDK unit test for ensureFolderLoaded (deep target, not-found, already-loaded); new web E2E: reload -> open bin -> restore an item whose subfolder parent was never navigated to.\n\nScope: SDK + web. Pre-existing, independent of the verified #494 hotfix."
}

---
created: 2026-02-24T23:30
title: Make search index build async/incremental for large vaults
area: ui

  - apps/web/src/services/search-index.service.ts:110-163
  - apps/web/src/hooks/useSearch.ts:79-128
---

## Problem

`buildFromFolderTree()` in SearchIndexService is synchronous and rebuilds the entire MiniSearch index on every palette open. It iterates all folders + children, deduplicates, then calls `miniSearch.addAll()` (tokenization + inverted index construction). For the current tech demonstrator scope (500MB quota, hundreds of files) this is sub-50ms, but for large vaults with thousands of files it could block the main thread and cause UI jank.

On PRs the \"Web E2E\" job does NOT run — PRs show only `SDK E2E Tests` + a `Verify E2E Passed` gate (which passes when web-e2e is skipped). So a web regression is first observed on the **post-merge push to main**, not on the PR.\n\n**Why it matters:** PR #494 (phase 47 SDK folder-state/publish consolidation) broke `writable-shares.spec.ts:330` (\"4.3 Alice edits Bob's file\") and it passed all PR checks, then failed Web E2E on the main push. Confirm regressions by comparing the failing commit to its parent's Web E2E run (`gh run list --workflow ci-e2e.yml --branch main`).\n\n**How to verify a web fix before merge:** push the branch and manually dispatch — `env -u GITHUB_TOKEN gh workflow run ci-e2e.yml --ref <branch>` (dispatch forces both web+desktop E2E via the `Force all changes on manual dispatch` step). Poll the \"Web E2E\" job specifically.\n\nRelated: [[project-web-sdk-folder-state-desync]], [[project-web-vitest-include-test-only]].\n"
}

42	- [PR-create triggers a bot release commit](project-gh-pr-create-bot-push.md) — after `gh pr create`, github-actions[bot] pushes `chore(release)` to the branch; the next local push is rejected until `git fetch` + `git rebase origin/<branch>`
43	- [gh pr edit fails on Projects-classic](project-gh-pr-edit-projects-classic-fails.md) — `gh pr edit` exits 1 (`projectCards` GraphQL deprecation) on this repo; patch PR body/title via `gh api -X PATCH repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/<N> -F body=@file`. Also: wrap bare `#NN` scope-item refs in backticks in PR bodies so GFM doesn't auto-link them as issues
44	
45	## CodeRabbit CLI
46	
47	- [Use --agent not --plain](feedback-coderabbit-cli-use-agent-flag.md) — `coderabbit review --agent` gives structured findings that are easier to parse than `--plain`; keep `--base-commit <sha>` to scope a single phase's diff
48	
49	## GSD Monorepo Gotchas
50	
51	- [Cross-package dist staleness](project-cross-package-dist-staleness.md) — sdk/web tsc check the built dist, not source; rebuild sdk-core/sdk dist before consumer typecheck after public-API changes
52	- [ROADMAP truncation by gsd-planner](project-roadmap-truncation-gsd-planner.md) — gap-mode planner edits can truncate ROADMAP.md; verify `wc -l` + phase count after any agent touches it, restore from git if broken
53	- [Web vitest runs *.test.ts only](project-web-vitest-include-test-only.md) — apps/web vitest `include` is `src/**/*.test.ts`; `.spec.ts` files (the API/jest convention) are silently skipped and never run in CI
54	- [Executor cargo fmt strands out-of-scope drift](project-gsd-executor-cargo-fmt-strands-drift.md) — Rust executors `cargo fmt` the whole package, leaving pre-existing fmt drift dirty; after execute-phase `git checkout --` the fmt-only out-of-scope files and re-verify the COMMITTED tree (check/test run on the working tree). Also verify deep-code-review "blockers" against pre/post source before acting
55	
56	## SDK / Web State
57	
58	- [Web/SDK folder-state desync](project-web-sdk-folder-state-desync.md) — folder state lives in BOTH Zustand `useFolderStore` and the SDK client `folderTree`; direct sdk-core publishes (file replace/version) advance only the store, leaving folderTree stale → stale-sequence 409 + merge resurrecting deleted files. Treat IPNS `sequenceNumber` as the version clock; reconcile folderTree before SDK mutations (PR #489)
59	- [SDK helper .mjs scripts drift untyped](project-sdk-helper-scripts-untyped-drift.md) — `packages/sdk-core/scripts/*.mjs` E2E helpers aren't typechecked/linted/unit-tested; they silently drift from SDK contract changes and only break in desktop cross-client-sync E2E on main. After sdk-core API changes, grep these scripts for changed symbols (PR #488 → #495 broke edit-filepointer with a 400)

## Where things stand

**1. #494 regression — fixed & verified ✅**
`fix(web): register folder in SDK folderTree before file edit and version writes` (`fc05bf87b`). Web E2E **passed green** on the branch run — `writable-shares 4.3` and the full suite.

**2. Comb-through — clean except one pre-existing gap**
21 call sites audited; only **bin restore** (`useBin.ts:73`/`:128`) shares the bug class. It's pre-existing (PR #296), not E2E-covered, and the fix needs a from-root resolver — so per your call it's **captured as a todo, not bundled** into the verified hotfix.

**3. Your architectural point — confirmed, and it's the real root cause**
The SDK client owns folder state but its config lacks the root IPNS private key, so it can't self-bootstrap or lazy-load — which is *why* the whole `'Folder not loaded'` class exists (`ensureFolderRegistered` is a web-side workaround, and #494 just dropped 3 of those calls). The captured todo proposes the proper fix: put `rootIpnsKeypair` in the config + internal `ensureFolderLoaded(ipnsName)` lazy walk, which dissolves the workaround entirely. (No new security exposure — the client already holds the vault key, root folder key, and per-folder IPNS keys in memory.)

Also recorded: a memory note that web-e2e only runs on main-push (so this class of regression can't be caught on a PR without a manual dispatch).

## Next step

The hotfix branch is ready for a PR. Note it currently carries two commits — the code fix `fc05bf87b` + the docs todo `d6ffb4bf0`; I can run `/gsd:pr-branch` to strip the `.planning/` commit for a code-only PR if you want it clean. Want me to open the PR?
